# bsc determinism gate: the allow-list (util/determinism/compare.py).
#
# Every file listed here is known to differ, after compare.py's normalisers,
# between a normal run of the testsuite and the same run with
# TEST_BSC_OPTIONS=-reverse-intern-order, because some order taken from Ord Id
# (string-intern order) reaches the file.  Each entry is tied to the root cause
# that still has to be fixed; the ids and their sites are in roots.md.
#
# Rules
#  - The list only shrinks.  Entries are removed when a determinism fix lands
#    with a test; nothing is added without a classified root (compare.py marks
#    new files UNCLASSIFIED and the gate goes red).
#  - A stale entry fails the gate: compare.py exits 2 when a listed file no
#    longer differs, is absent from both archives, or is shadowed by ignore.txt.
#  - Test-harness noise does not belong here; it goes in ignore.txt with a
#    reason, and dated headers, VCD $date sections and vvp addresses are
#    normalised by compare.py.
#
# Format: TAB-separated  path<TAB>root_id<TAB>note, sorted by path; paths are
# relative to testsuite/ with no leading './'.  The note names the kind of file
# first, then any further roots the file also carries ("also ..."): an entry
# with several roots stays until all of them are fixed.  Special root_ids:
#    only-one-side  the file exists in only one archive (its cause is in the note)
#    pending-ba     .ba whose plain-dumpba text is identical in both runs: the
#                   bytes differ only through the Id-ordered Map/Set encoding
#                   (BinData.hs:640-645); no classifier root assigned yet
#    pending-*      no classifier record for the file
#
# Baseline: bsc 575feb17 (every determinism fix to date, plus the change that
# stops recording -reverse-intern-order in the .ba), archives of 2026-10-03
# (normal vs reversed), classified by root in roots.md.  Regenerate with
# compare.py --write-allowlist after a fix, and review the diff: it may only
# remove lines.
